02:25:53 --> 02:25:59
someone comes to me and says they're

02:25:54 --> 02:26:01

02:26:08 --> 02:26:13

02:26:14 --> 02:26:20

02:26:26 --> 02:26:31

02:26:28 --> 02:26:33

02:26:34 --> 02:26:40

02:26:39 --> 02:26:45

02:26:41 --> 02:26:47

02:26:44 --> 02:26:50

02:26:46 --> 02:26:51
fundamental sense and all that's working
